\index{idt8a340\_reg.h@{idt8a340\_reg.h}!Q10\_TO\_Q11\_FANOUT\_AND\_CLOCK\_SYNC\_ENABLE\_MASK@{Q10\_TO\_Q11\_FANOUT\_AND\_CLOCK\_SYNC\_ENABLE\_MASK}}
\index{Q10\_TO\_Q11\_FANOUT\_AND\_CLOCK\_SYNC\_ENABLE\_MASK@{Q10\_TO\_Q11\_FANOUT\_AND\_CLOCK\_SYNC\_ENABLE\_MASK}!idt8a340\_reg.h@{idt8a340\_reg.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Q10\_TO\_Q11\_FANOUT\_AND\_CLOCK\_SYNC\_ENABLE\_MASK}{Q10\_TO\_Q11\_FANOUT\_AND\_CLOCK\_SYNC\_ENABLE\_MASK}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define Q10\+\_\+\+T\+O\+\_\+\+Q11\+\_\+\+F\+A\+N\+O\+U\+T\+\_\+\+A\+N\+D\+\_\+\+C\+L\+O\+C\+K\+\_\+\+S\+Y\+N\+C\+\_\+\+E\+N\+A\+B\+L\+E\+\_\+\+M\+A\+SK~(\mbox{\hyperlink{vdso_2bits_8h_a4eaf4bf81c52c3f375967906e4d5ba17}{B\+IT}}(0) $\vert$ \mbox{\hyperlink{vdso_2bits_8h_a4eaf4bf81c52c3f375967906e4d5ba17}{B\+IT}}(2))}

Enable F\+O\+D6 as driver for clock and sync for Q11 divider. Enable fanout buffer for F\+O\+D6.

Unused when F\+O\+D7 is driving Q11 divider (normal operation). \mbox{\Hypertarget{idt8a340__reg_8h_a03782c2c07e7a050c38ab79282dfb6b7}\label{idt8a340__reg_8h_a03782c2c07e7a050c38ab79282dfb6b7}} 
\index{idt8a340\_reg.h@{idt8a340\_reg.h}!Q10\_TO\_Q11\_SYNC\_TRIG@{Q10\_TO\_Q11\_SYNC\_TRIG}}
\index{Q10\_TO\_Q11\_SYNC\_TRIG@{Q10\_TO\_Q11\_SYNC\_TRIG}!idt8a340\_reg.h@{idt8a340\_reg.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Q10\_TO\_Q11\_SYNC\_TRIG}{Q10\_TO\_Q11\_SYNC\_TRIG}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define Q10\+\_\+\+T\+O\+\_\+\+Q11\+\_\+\+S\+Y\+N\+C\+\_\+\+T\+R\+IG~\mbox{\hyperlink{vdso_2bits_8h_a4eaf4bf81c52c3f375967906e4d5ba17}{B\+IT}}(1)}

Select F\+O\+D6 as sync\+\_\+trigger for Q11 divider. Transition from logic zero to one sets trigger to sync Q11 divider.

Unused when F\+O\+D7 is driving Q11 divider (normal operation). \mbox{\Hypertarget{idt8a340__reg_8h_aaed1a2de3b09f26775e76f11dd5cfa21}\label{idt8a340__reg_8h_aaed1a2de3b09f26775e76f11dd5cfa21}} 
\index{idt8a340\_reg.h@{idt8a340\_reg.h}!Q9\_TO\_Q8\_FANOUT\_AND\_CLOCK\_SYNC\_ENABLE\_MASK@{Q9\_TO\_Q8\_FANOUT\_AND\_CLOCK\_SYNC\_ENABLE\_MASK}}
\index{Q9\_TO\_Q8\_FANOUT\_AND\_CLOCK\_SYNC\_ENABLE\_MASK@{Q9\_TO\_Q8\_FANOUT\_AND\_CLOCK\_SYNC\_ENABLE\_MASK}!idt8a340\_reg.h@{idt8a340\_reg.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Q9\_TO\_Q8\_FANOUT\_AND\_CLOCK\_SYNC\_ENABLE\_MASK}{Q9\_TO\_Q8\_FANOUT\_AND\_CLOCK\_SYNC\_ENABLE\_MASK}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define Q9\+\_\+\+T\+O\+\_\+\+Q8\+\_\+\+F\+A\+N\+O\+U\+T\+\_\+\+A\+N\+D\+\_\+\+C\+L\+O\+C\+K\+\_\+\+S\+Y\+N\+C\+\_\+\+E\+N\+A\+B\+L\+E\+\_\+\+M\+A\+SK~(\mbox{\hyperlink{vdso_2bits_8h_a4eaf4bf81c52c3f375967906e4d5ba17}{B\+IT}}(0) $\vert$ \mbox{\hyperlink{vdso_2bits_8h_a4eaf4bf81c52c3f375967906e4d5ba17}{B\+IT}}(2))}

Enable F\+O\+D5 as driver for clock and sync for Q8 divider. Enable fanout buffer for F\+O\+D5.

Unused when F\+O\+D4 is driving Q8 divider (normal operation). \mbox{\Hypertarget{idt8a340__reg_8h_aecc13772b57e69a1a5e563238f968bd2}\label{idt8a340__reg_8h_aecc13772b57e69a1a5e563238f968bd2}} 
\index{idt8a340\_reg.h@{idt8a340\_reg.h}!Q9\_TO\_Q8\_SYNC\_TRIG@{Q9\_TO\_Q8\_SYNC\_TRIG}}
\index{Q9\_TO\_Q8\_SYNC\_TRIG@{Q9\_TO\_Q8\_SYNC\_TRIG}!idt8a340\_reg.h@{idt8a340\_reg.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{Q9\_TO\_Q8\_SYNC\_TRIG}{Q9\_TO\_Q8\_SYNC\_TRIG}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \#define Q9\+\_\+\+T\+O\+\_\+\+Q8\+\_\+\+S\+Y\+N\+C\+\_\+\+T\+R\+IG~\mbox{\hyperlink{vdso_2bits_8h_a4eaf4bf81c52c3f375967906e4d5ba17}{B\+IT}}(1)}

Select F\+O\+D5 as sync\+\_\+trigger for Q8 divider. Transition from logic zero to one sets trigger to sync Q8 divider.

Unused when F\+O\+D4 is driving Q8 divider (normal operation). \mbox{\Hypertarget{idt8a340__reg_8h_a6c6e2af0b09ac4055622b4c8f4fbf9eb}\label{idt8a340__reg_8h_a6c6e2af0b09ac4055622b4c8f4fbf9eb}}
